# Vector search like Redis 8's bundled RediSearch 8.10.1 (VecSim): distances bit for bit on the
# reference's aarch64 kernels, KNN / hybrid / range queries, FT.INFO memory, profiles, cursors.
# Every index is created on an empty keyspace. No SVS-VAMANA index ever gets a vector: its shared
# thread pool would add to every later FT.INFO's vector memory on the reference.

FLUSHALL
# KNN basics: hash vectors, reply shapes, ordering, AS and $yield_distance_as, k edge cases.
FT.CREATE v ON HASH PREFIX 1 d: SCHEMA t TEXT g TAG n NUMERIC SORTABLE v VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC L2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
HSET d:1 t "hello world" g a n 0 v "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x00"
HSET d:2 t "hello there" g b n 1 v "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x40"
HSET d:3 t "foo bar" g a,b n 2 v "\x00\x00\x40\x40\x00\x00\x40\x40"
HSET d:4 t "hello foo" g c n 3 v "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f"
HSET d:5 t "bar baz" g a n 4 v "\x00\x00\x80\xbf\x00\x00\x00\x00"
HSET d:6 t "world" g b n 5 v "\x00\x00\x00\x40\x00\x00\x00\xc0"
HSET d:7 t "hello hello" g c n 6 v "\x00\x00\x00\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x3f"
HSET d:8 t "baz" g a n 7 v "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x00"
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score DESC DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 2 __v_score t DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHSCORES RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score LIMIT 1 2 RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score WITHSORTKEYS RETURN 1 t DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY n DESC RETURN 2 n __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B AS dist]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY dist RETURN 1 dist DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B AS dist]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: dd}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY dd RETURN 1 dd DIALECT 2

FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: x; $HYBRID_POLICY: ADHOC_BF}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" pol BATCHES SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@g:{a|b}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: $pol}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" pol BATCHES SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: $pol}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" pol BATCHES SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B HYBRID_POLICY BATCHES]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@g:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B HYBRID_POLICY BATCHES BATCH_SIZE 1]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@g:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B BATCH_SIZE $bs]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" bs 2 SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@g:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B BATCH_SIZE $bs]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@g:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B EF_RUNTIME 5]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@g:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B NOPE 5]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
# Range queries.
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 0 $B]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" r 2.5 NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 1 $B]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" r 2.5 NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 1.0001 $B]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" r 2.5 NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" r 2.5 NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 8 $B]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" r 2.5 NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 1e10 $B]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" r 2.5 NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE $r $B]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" r 2.5 NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE -1 $B]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" r 2.5 NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE -0 $B]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" r 2.5 NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY d RETURN 1 d DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 d DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHSCORES RETURN 1 d DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHSCORES RETURN 1 n DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d; $EPSILON: 0.5}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$BATCH_SIZE: 3}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$EF_RUNTIME: 3}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x40\x00\x00\x40\x40" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B] @g:{a}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d} @g:{a}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 d DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d} | @g:{c}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 2 d g DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "-@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d} @v:[VECTOR_RANGE 3 $C]=>{$yield_distance_as: e}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" C "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f" RETURN 2 d e DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d} @v:[VECTOR_RANGE 3 $C]=>{$yield_distance_as: d}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" C "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f" RETURN 1 d DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: t}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "(@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d})=>[KNN 2 @v $C]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" C "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f" RETURN 2 d __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "(@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d})=>[KNN 2 @v $C]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" C "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 2 d __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "(@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: __v_score})=>[KNN 2 @v $C]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" C "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "(@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B])=>[KNN 2 @v $C]" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" C "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
# FT.AGGREGATE.
FT.AGGREGATE v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY 2 @__v_score ASC DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" LOAD 1 @t SORTBY 2 @__v_score DESC DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B AS d]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" LOAD 2 @t @n APPLY "@d * 2" AS dd SORTBY 2 @d ASC DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "*=>[KNN 8 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" LOAD 1 @g GROUPBY 1 @g REDUCE MIN 1 @__v_score AS m REDUCE COUNT 0 AS c SORTBY 2 @g ASC DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" ADDSCORES SORTBY 2 @__v_score ASC DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "hello=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" ADDSCORES SORTBY 2 @__v_score ASC DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "hello=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHSCORES SORTBY 2 @__v_score ASC DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "hello=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" LOAD 1 @__v_score DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B AS n]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" APPLY 1 AS __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY 2 @__v_score ASC LIMIT 1 2 DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH v "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score
FT.DROPINDEX v
FLUSHALL
# FT.INFO vector memory: FLAT exact; an index is created by its first vector
FT.CREATE mf ON HASH PREFIX 1 mf: SCHEMA v VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 3 DISTANCE_METRIC L2 t TAG
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mf
HSET mf:0 t a
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mf
HSET mf:1 v "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x3f\x00\x00\x80\xbf"
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mf
HSET mf:2 v "\x00\x00\x00\x40\x00\x00\x00\x3f\x00\x00\x00\xc0"
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mf
HSET mf:3 v "\x00\x00\x40\x40\x00\x00\x00\x3f\x00\x00\x40\xc0"
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mf
HSET mf:1 v "\x00\x00\x10\x41\x00\x00\x10\x41\x00\x00\x10\x41"
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mf
HSET mf:1 v short
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mf
DEL mf:1
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mf
DEL mf:2
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mf
DEL mf:3
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mf
HSET mf:5 v "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x40\x00\x00\x40\x40"
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mf
FT.DROPINDEX mf
FLUSHALL

HSET mt:2 v "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x40\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x08\x40\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x10\x40\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x14\x40"

!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mt

# multi-value: a lookup entry per document, its ids growing by doubling
FLUSHALL
FT.CREATE mj ON JSON PREFIX 1 mj: SCHEMA $.v[*] AS v VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC L2
JSON.SET mj:1 $ '{"v": [[0, 1]]}'
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
JSON.SET mj:2 $ '{"v": [[0, 2], [1, 2]]}'
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
JSON.SET mj:3 $ '{"v": [[0, 3], [1, 3], [2, 3]]}'
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
JSON.SET mj:5 $ '{"v": [[0, 5], [1, 5], [2, 5], [3, 5], [4, 5]]}'
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
JSON.SET mj:9 $ '{"v": [[0, 9], [1, 9], [2, 9], [3, 9], [4, 9], [5, 9], [6, 9], [7, 9], [8, 9]]}'
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
DEL mj:3
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
JSON.SET mj:1 $ '{"v": [[7, 7], [8, 8]]}'
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
DEL mj:1
DEL mj:2
DEL mj:5
DEL mj:9
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
# blocks of 1024 vectors: data blocks, idToLabelMapping and the lookup buckets grow and shrink by block
EVAL "local t = {} for i = 0, 999 do t[#t + 1] = '[' .. i .. ',0]' end return redis.call('JSON.SET', KEYS[1], '$', '{\"v\":[' .. table.concat(t, ',') .. ']}')" 1 mj:b0
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
EVAL "local t = {} for i = 0, 999 do t[#t + 1] = '[' .. i .. ',1]' end return redis.call('JSON.SET', KEYS[1], '$', '{\"v\":[' .. table.concat(t, ',') .. ']}')" 1 mj:b1
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
EVAL "local t = {} for i = 0, 999 do t[#t + 1] = '[' .. i .. ',2]' end return redis.call('JSON.SET', KEYS[1], '$', '{\"v\":[' .. table.concat(t, ',') .. ']}')" 1 mj:b2
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
EVAL "local t = {} for i = 0, 999 do t[#t + 1] = '[' .. i .. ',3]' end return redis.call('JSON.SET', KEYS[1], '$', '{\"v\":[' .. table.concat(t, ',') .. ']}')" 1 mj:b3
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
JSON.SET mj:c $ '{"v": [[0, 9], [1, 9], [2, 9], [3, 9], [4, 9], [5, 9], [6, 9], [7, 9], [8, 9], [9, 9], [10, 9], [11, 9], [12, 9], [13, 9], [14, 9], [15, 9], [16, 9], [17, 9], [18, 9], [19, 9], [20, 9], [21, 9], [22, 9], [23, 9], [24, 9], [25, 9], [26, 9], [27, 9], [28, 9], [29, 9]]}'
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
DEL mj:b1
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
DEL mj:b3
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
DEL mj:b0
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
DEL mj:c
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
DEL mj:b2
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mj
FT.DROPINDEX mj
FLUSHALL
FT.CREATE ms ON HASH PREFIX 1 ms: SCHEMA v VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 1 DISTANCE_METRIC L2
EVAL "for i = 1, 1022 do redis.call('HSET', 'ms:' .. i, 'v', struct.pack('<f', i)) end return 1" 0
HSET ms:1023 v "\x00\xc0\x7f\x44"

!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO ms
DEL ms:3

# HNSW: the graph after background ingestion
FLUSHALL
FT.CREATE mh ON HASH PREFIX 1 mh: SCHEMA v VECTOR HNSW 8 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 3 DISTANCE_METRIC L2 M 2
HSET mh:1 v "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x00"
@sleep 50
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mh
HSET mh:2 v "\x00\x00\x00\x40\x00\x00\x00\x40\x00\x00\x00\x00"
@sleep 50
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mh
HSET mh:3 v "\x00\x00\x40\x40\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00"
@sleep 50
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mh
HSET mh:4 v "\x00\x00\x80\x40\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x00"
@sleep 50
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mh

@sleep 50
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mh

@sleep 50
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO mh

# An index without vectors yet: no vector index, every vector query is empty.
FT.CREATE e ON HASH PREFIX 1 e: SCHEMA t TAG v VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC L2
HSET e:1 t a
FT.SEARCH e "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH e "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B abc DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE e "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH e "@t:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH e "@t:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B abc DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE e "@t:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH e "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 1 $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH e "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 1 $B]" PARAMS 2 B abc DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE e "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 1 $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH e "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]=>{$EF_RUNTIME: 0}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH e "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]=>{$EF_RUNTIME: 0}" PARAMS 2 B abc DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE e "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]=>{$EF_RUNTIME: 0}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH e "@t:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: nope}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH e "@t:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: nope}" PARAMS 2 B abc DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE e "@t:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: nope}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH e "*=>[KNN 300000000000000000 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH e "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE -1 $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO e
# Indexing failures: a blob of the wrong size.
HSET e:2 t b v abc
HSET e:3 t c v "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f"
HSET e:4 v "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f"

HSET e:3 v ab
FT.SEARCH e "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH e "*" NOCONTENT
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO e
FT.DROPINDEX e
FLUSHALL
# Updates and deletes: overwritten, removed and re-added vectors.
FT.CREATE u ON HASH PREFIX 1 u: SCHEMA t TAG v VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC L2 w VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC IP
HSET u:1 t b v "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x00" w "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x80\x3f"

HDEL u:5 v

FT.SEARCH u "*=>[KNN 4 @w $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f" SORTBY __w_score RETURN 1 __w_score DIALECT 2
DEL u:1

FT.SEARCH u "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 10 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY d RETURN 1 d DIALECT 2

# JSON: arrays of numbers, multi-value paths, indexing failures.
FT.CREATE j ON JSON PREFIX 1 j: SCHEMA $.v AS v VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 3 DISTANCE_METRIC L2 $.d AS d VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT64 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC COSINE $.i AS i VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE INT8 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC IP $.h AS h VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT16 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC L2 $.n AS n NUMERIC
JSON.SET j:1 $ '{"v":[1,2,3],"d":[0.5,0.25],"i":[3,-4],"h":[0.1,0.2],"n":1}'
JSON.SET j:2 $ '{"v":[1.5,-2,0.125],"d":[1,1],"i":[127,-128],"h":[65504,-1],"n":2}'
JSON.SET j:3 $ '{"v":[0,0,0],"d":[-3,4],"i":[0,1],"h":[0.0001,3],"n":3}'
JSON.SET j:4 $ '{"v":[1,2],"n":4}'
JSON.SET j:5 $ '{"v":"abc","n":5}'
JSON.SET j:6 $ '{"v":[1,"a",3],"n":6}'
JSON.SET j:7 $ '{"v":[[1,2,3]],"n":7}'
JSON.SET j:8 $ '{"i":[300,1],"n":8}'
JSON.SET j:9 $ '{"i":[1.5,1],"n":9}'
JSON.SET j:10 $ '{"v":[1e+40,0,0],"n":10}'
JSON.SET j:11 $ '{"v":[true,0,0],"n":11}'
JSON.SET j:12 $ '{"v":[],"n":12}'
JSON.SET j:13 $ '{"v":null,"n":13}'
JSON.SET j:14 $ '{"d":[1e+308,1e+308],"h":[1000000.0,0],"n":14}'
FT.SEARCH j "*" NOCONTENT SORTBY n
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO j
FT.SEARCH j "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH j "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f" SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH j "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 2 $.v __v_score DIALECT 3
FT.SEARCH j "*=>[KNN 5 @d $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\xf0\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __d_score RETURN 1 __d_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH j "*=>[KNN 5 @i $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x01\x01" SORTBY __i_score RETURN 1 __i_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH j "*=>[KNN 5 @h $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __h_score RETURN 1 __h_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH j "@n:[1 2]=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE j "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f" LOAD 1 @n SORTBY 2 @__v_score ASC DIALECT 2
FT.DROPINDEX j
FLUSHALL
FT.CREATE jm ON JSON PREFIX 1 jm: SCHEMA $.v[*] AS v VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC L2 $.w AS w VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC L2 $..x AS x VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC IP $.n AS n NUMERIC

FT.SEARCH h "@t:{a}=>[KNN 2 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: ADHOC_BF; $EF_RUNTIME: 10}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "*=>[KNN 2 @v $B]=>{$EF_RUNTIME: 10; $HYBRID_POLICY: ADHOC_BF}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2

FT.SEARCH h "*=>[KNN 2 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $EF_RUNTIME: 30; $BATCH_SIZE: 20}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@t:{a}=>[KNN 2 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $EF_RUNTIME: 30; $BATCH_SIZE: 20}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$EPSILON: 0}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$EPSILON: -1}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$EPSILON: abc}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$EPSILON: 1e400}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$EPSILON: 0.1; $EPSILON: 0.2}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$EF_RUNTIME: 10}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$BATCH_SIZE: 2}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: ADHOC_BF}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$EPSILON: 0x10}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$EPSILON: inf}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$EPSILON: infinity}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$EPSILON: 1e-310}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 5 $B]=>{$EPSILON: $e}" PARAMS 4 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" e " 0.5" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
!mask total_indexing_time,memory,vector_index_sz_mb,total_index_memory_sz_mb FT.INFO h
DEL h:1 h:4
HSET h:2 v "\x00\x00\x80\x3e\x00\x00\x80\x3e"
@sleep 100
FT.SEARCH h "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "*=>[KNN 20 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH h "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 30 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY d RETURN 1 d DIALECT 2
!mask total_indexing_time,memory,vector_index_sz_mb,total_index_memory_sz_mb FT.INFO h
!timing FT.PROFILE h SEARCH QUERY "*=>[KNN 20 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE h SEARCH QUERY "@t:{a}=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE h SEARCH QUERY "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 30 $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.DROPINDEX h
FLUSHALL
FT.CREATE hc ON JSON PREFIX 1 hc: SCHEMA $.v[*] AS v VECTOR HNSW 6 TYPE FLOAT64 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC COSINE
JSON.SET hc:1 $ '{"v":[[0.54,0.841],[0.841,-0.54]]}'
JSON.SET hc:2 $ '{"v":[[-0.832,1.819],[1.819,0.832]]}'
JSON.SET hc:3 $ '{"v":[[-2.97,0.423],[0.423,2.97]]}'
JSON.SET hc:4 $ '{"v":[[-2.615,-3.027],[-3.027,2.615]]}'
JSON.SET hc:5 $ '{"v":[[1.418,-4.795],[-4.795,-1.418]]}'
JSON.SET hc:6 $ '{"v":[[5.761,-1.676],[-1.676,-5.761]]}'
@sleep 100
FT.SEARCH hc "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\xf0\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH hc "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 0.2 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\xf0\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY d RETURN 1 d DIALECT 2
!mask total_indexing_time,memory,vector_index_sz_mb,total_index_memory_sz_mb FT.INFO hc
FT.DROPINDEX hc
FLUSHALL
# SVS-VAMANA: the index exists from the first vector on; without vectors queries are empty.
FT.CREATE sv ON HASH PREFIX 1 sv: SCHEMA t TAG v VECTOR SVS-VAMANA 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC L2
HSET sv:1 t a
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO sv
FT.SEARCH sv "*=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH sv "*=>[KNN 2 @v $B]=>{$SEARCH_WINDOW_SIZE: 10}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH sv "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 2 $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE sv SEARCH QUERY "*=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE sv SEARCH QUERY "@t:{a}=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.DROPINDEX sv
FLUSHALL
# FT.PROFILE: the vector iterators (VECTOR with its search mode and batches, METRIC for ranges
# yielding distances, else an ID list) and the Metrics Applier.
FT.CREATE p ON HASH PREFIX 1 p: SCHEMA t TEXT g TAG n NUMERIC v VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC L2
HSET p:1 t "foo" g b n 1 v "\x00\x00\x00\x3f\x00\x00\x00\x40"
HSET p:2 t "hello" g c n 2 v "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x40"
HSET p:3 t "hello world" g a n 3 v "\x00\x00\xc0\x3f\x00\x00\x80\x3f"
HSET p:4 t "foo" g b n 4 v "\x00\x00\x00\x40\x00\x00\x40\x40"
HSET p:5 t "hello" g c n 5 v "\x00\x00\x20\x40\x00\x00\x00\x00"
HSET p:6 t "hello world" g a n 6 v "\x00\x00\x40\x40\x00\x00\x00\x40"
HSET p:7 t "foo" g b n 7 v "\x00\x00\x60\x40\x00\x00\x80\x40"
HSET p:8 t "hello" g c n 8 v "\x00\x00\x80\x40\x00\x00\x80\x3f"
HSET p:9 t "hello world" g a n 9 v "\x00\x00\x90\x40\x00\x00\x40\x40"
HSET p:10 t "foo" g b n 10 v "\x00\x00\xa0\x40\x00\x00\x00\x00"
HSET p:11 t "hello" g c n 11 v "\x00\x00\xb0\x40\x00\x00\x00\x40"
HSET p:12 t "hello world" g a n 12 v "\x00\x00\xc0\x40\x00\x00\x80\x40"

!timing FT.PROFILE p SEARCH LIMITED QUERY "@n:[1 6]=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE p AGGREGATE QUERY "@n:[1 6]=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE p SEARCH QUERY "@t:hello @n:[1 6]=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE p SEARCH LIMITED QUERY "@t:hello @n:[1 6]=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE p AGGREGATE QUERY "@t:hello @n:[1 6]=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE p SEARCH QUERY "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE p SEARCH QUERY "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY n LIMIT 0 2 DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE p SEARCH QUERY "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHSCORES DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE p SEARCH QUERY "hello=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHSCORES NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE p AGGREGATE QUERY "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY 2 @__v_score ASC DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE p AGGREGATE QUERY "hello=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" ADDSCORES SORTBY 2 @__v_score ASC DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE p AGGREGATE QUERY "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" LOAD 1 @n APPLY "@__v_score+1" AS s DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE p AGGREGATE QUERY "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 4
!timing FT.PROFILE p SEARCH QUERY "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 4
# Scores of hybrid queries: the text score passes through the vector iterator.
FT.SEARCH p "hello=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SCORER TFIDF WITHSCORES RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH p "hello=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SCORER TFIDF.DOCNORM WITHSCORES RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH p "hello=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SCORER BM25 WITHSCORES RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH p "hello=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SCORER BM25STD WITHSCORES RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH p "hello=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SCORER DISMAX WITHSCORES RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH p "hello=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SCORER DOCSCORE WITHSCORES RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH p "hello=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SCORER HAMMING WITHSCORES RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH p "hello=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHSCORES EXPLAINSCORE RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH p "hello=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SCORER BM25STD WITHSCORES EXPLAINSCORE RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH p "*=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHSCORES EXPLAINSCORE RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH p "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 3 $B] hello" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHSCORES EXPLAINSCORE NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH p "(hello | foo)=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHSCORES SCORER BM25 RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE p "hello=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SCORER BM25 ADDSCORES SORTBY 2 @__v_score ASC DIALECT 2
# Cursors over vector queries.
!capture c FT.AGGREGATE p "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHCURSOR COUNT 2 DIALECT 2
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
!capture c FT.AGGREGATE p "@g:{a|b}=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHCURSOR COUNT 2 DIALECT 2
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
!capture c FT.AGGREGATE p "@g:{a|b}=>[KNN 5 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $BATCH_SIZE: 2}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" LOAD 1 @n WITHCURSOR COUNT 2 DIALECT 2
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
!capture c FT.AGGREGATE p "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 20 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHCURSOR COUNT 3 DIALECT 2
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
!capture c FT.AGGREGATE p "*=>[KNN 6 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY 2 @__v_score DESC WITHCURSOR COUNT 4 DIALECT 2
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
!capture c FT.AGGREGATE p "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHCURSOR COUNT 2 DIALECT 2
HSET p:100 g a n 100 v "\x0a\xd7\x23\x3c\x00\x00\x00\x00"
DEL p:1
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
!capture c FT.AGGREGATE p "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHCURSOR COUNT 1 DIALECT 2
DEL p:3
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.CURSOR READ p {$c}
FT.DROPINDEX p
FLUSHALL

# RESP3 replies and paging.
FT.CREATE r ON HASH PREFIX 1 r: SCHEMA t TEXT g TAG v VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 2 DISTANCE_METRIC L2
HSET r:1 t "world hello" g b v "\x00\x00\x80\x3f\x00\x00\x00\xbf"
HSET r:2 t "foo" g a v "\x00\x00\x00\x40\x00\x00\x80\xbf"
HSET r:3 t "hello" g b v "\x00\x00\x40\x40\x00\x00\xc0\xbf"
HSET r:4 t "world hello" g a v "\x00\x00\x80\x40\x00\x00\x00\xc0"
HSET r:5 t "foo" g b v "\x00\x00\xa0\x40\x00\x00\x20\xc0"
HSET r:6 t "hello" g a v "\x00\x00\xc0\x40\x00\x00\x40\xc0"
HSET r:7 t "world hello" g b v "\x00\x00\xe0\x40\x00\x00\x60\xc0"
@resp 3
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score WITHSCORES DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "hello=>[KNN 3 @v $B AS d]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY d WITHSCORES RETURN 1 d DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 20 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY d RETURN 1 d DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE r "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY 2 @__v_score ASC DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE r "@g:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" LOAD 1 @t DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE r SEARCH QUERY "@g:{a}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE r AGGREGATE QUERY "@v:[VECTOR_RANGE 20 $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B abc DIALECT 2
@resp 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" LIMIT 0 0 DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" LIMIT 3 10 NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" LIMIT 10 10 NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score LIMIT 1 2 NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 0 DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 v DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" INKEYS 2 r:1 r:3 RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" INFIELDS 1 t RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "hello=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" INFIELDS 1 t RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" HIGHLIGHT RETURN 1 t DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "hello=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" HIGHLIGHT RETURN 1 t DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY t RETURN 2 t __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" TIMEOUT 0 RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" VERBATIM RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 2 @v $B] @g:{a}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "@g:{a} *=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "(@g:{a})=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 __v_score SORTBY __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "@g:{a}=>[KNN 2 @v $B]=>{$yield_distance_as: d}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 d SORTBY d DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" DIALECT 1
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 3
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 4
FT.SEARCH r "*=>[KNN 2 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHCOUNT RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 4
FT.AGGREGATE r "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY 2 @__v_score ASC MAX 2 DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE r "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" FILTER "@__v_score < 10" DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE r "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" GROUPBY 1 @g REDUCE MIN 1 @__v_score AS l REDUCE COUNT 0 AS c SORTBY 2 @g ASC DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE r "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" LOAD * SORTBY 2 @__v_score ASC DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE r "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" WITHCOUNT DIALECT 2
FT.AGGREGATE r "*=>[KNN 4 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00" LIMIT 0 2 DIALECT 2
FT.DROPINDEX r
FLUSHALL
# Brute force batches over 2000+ vectors: heap-based selection (remaining / 1000 above the batch size), with distinct and tied distances.
FT.CREATE ms ON HASH PREFIX 1 ms: SCHEMA t TAG v VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 1 DISTANCE_METRIC L2
EVAL "for i = 1, 2100 do redis.call('HSET', 'ms:' .. i, 'v', struct.pack('<f', (i * 7919) % 2100 + 0.5), 't', tostring(i % 50)) end return 1" 0
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO ms
FT.SEARCH ms "@t:{3}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $BATCH_SIZE: 1}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH ms "@t:{3|4}=>[KNN 5 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $BATCH_SIZE: 2}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE ms SEARCH QUERY "@t:{3}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $BATCH_SIZE: 1}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE ms SEARCH QUERY "@t:{3|4}=>[KNN 5 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $BATCH_SIZE: 2}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
!timing FT.PROFILE ms SEARCH QUERY "@t:{3}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" NOCONTENT DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH ms "*=>[KNN 3 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
EVAL "for i = 1, 1100 do redis.call('DEL', 'ms:' .. i) end return 1" 0
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO ms
EVAL "for i = 1101, 2100 do redis.call('DEL', 'ms:' .. i) end return 1" 0
!mask total_indexing_time FT.INFO ms
FT.DROPINDEX ms
FLUSHALL
FT.CREATE ms ON HASH PREFIX 1 ms: SCHEMA t TAG v VECTOR FLAT 6 TYPE FLOAT32 DIM 1 DISTANCE_METRIC L2
EVAL "for i = 1, 2100 do redis.call('HSET', 'ms:' .. i, 'v', struct.pack('<f', i % 10), 't', tostring(i % 50)) end return 1" 0
FT.SEARCH ms "@t:{3}=>[KNN 3 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $BATCH_SIZE: 1}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH ms "@t:{3|4|10}=>[KNN 5 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $BATCH_SIZE: 2}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" SORTBY __v_score RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH ms "@t:{3|4|10}=>[KNN 5 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $BATCH_SIZE: 2}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH ms "@t:{3|4|10|20}=>[KNN 8 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $BATCH_SIZE: 1}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH ms "@t:{3|4|10|20}=>[KNN 8 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $BATCH_SIZE: 30}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH ms "@t:{3|4|10|20}=>[KNN 8 @v $B]=>{$HYBRID_POLICY: BATCHES; $BATCH_SIZE: 300}" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH ms "*=>[KNN 5 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.SEARCH ms "@t:{3|4|10|20}=>[KNN 8 @v $B]" PARAMS 2 B "\x00\x00\x00\x00" RETURN 1 __v_score DIALECT 2
FT.DROPINDEX ms
FLUSHALL
